            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 0 (explicit refutation) conflicts with external claim 1; blockade stands per the original refutation")
    elif act == 1:
        if ext == 0:
            act_item, src = 0, "external refutation"
            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 1 conflicts with external claim 0; conservative blockade")
        else:
            act_item, src = 1, "F atom"
    else:
        if ext is None:
            act_item, src = "⊥", "unproven"
        elif ext == "⊥":
            act_item, src = "⊥", "explicitly unproven"
        else:
            act_item, src = ext, "explicit certification"
    detail, certs, witness, wfour, first_four = [], [], None, None, None
    for k in fam:
        ev = k["evidence"]
        # actual difference set: keep only the positions whose value really differs from the pre-beat value (setting a position back to its pre-beat value is not a change)
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        legal = all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set)
        s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
        dk = abs(f_component(law, s2) - c)
        # the route is bound to the bearing positions this contrast actually changes: the path from these starting points to this event; the aggregate Path does not replace it
        bearers = frozenset(i for i in act_set if i in avail)
        p_k = path_from(bearers) if bearers else 0
        route_k = ev.get("route", evidence.get("route", 1 if p_k == 1 else "⊥"))
        four_k = {"same-beat": ev.get("same-beat", evidence.get("same-beat", 1)),
                  "bearing position": ev.get("bearing position", evidence.get("bearing position", 1)) if legal else 0,
                  "route": route_k, "action": act_item}
        ck = kleene_and([1 if dk > eps else 0] + list(four_k.values()))
        detail.append((k["id"], dk, ck, dict(four_k)))
        if first_four is None:
            first_four = dict(four_k)
        certs.append(ck)
        if ck == 1 and witness is None:
            witness, wfour = k["id"], dict(four_k)
    cert = kleene_or(certs)
    four = wfour if witness is not None else (first_four if first_four is not None else
           {"same-beat": evidence.get("same-beat", 1), "bearing position": 0, "route": "⊥", "action": act_item})
    status = ("witness contrast “%s”" % witness if witness is not None else
              "disjunction of listed contrast certificates = %s" % cert + ("; declared domain not yet covered" if cover != "complete" else ""))
    atoms = {"Avail": 1 if avail else 0, "cert_read": cert_read, "Path": path, "HistCond_F": hf,
             "Act_F": act, "numeric difference": nd, "Def. 6′ four clauses": four, "action source": src, "evidence conflict": conflicts,
             "contrast details": detail, "witness contrast": witness, "complete certificate": cert, "coverage": cover, "certificate status": status}
    if independent is not None:
        need = ("event identity", "mode", "certification basis", "action")
        atoms["independent witness"] = dict(independent) if all(k in independent for k in need) else "independent witness incomplete"
    if cert == 1:
        col = "effective difference certified"
    elif hf == 1 or nd == "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold":
        col = "functional support established"
    else:
        col = "effective difference not certified"
    return atoms, col

def f_post_match(post, d, raw_sum, f_sum):
    """Comparison under the actual equation in Theorem 18-4(4)(A): one original-mechanism beat post(d + Σorig) against one F-layer beat d + ΣF."""
    return post(d + raw_sum) == d + f_sum

# ---- J₁₈: verification through the [S2] F-layer evaluator, trajectory reproduction and qualification (Theorem 18-4(4)) ----
def _tri(v):
    return {1: True, 0: False, "⊥": None, True: True, False: False, None: None}[v]

def _untri(v):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: "⊥"}[v]

def j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """Compiles the declared events of one run of this paper into the Spec of [S2] Appendix A.1 (raw events, certifications, times and sources are passed explicitly, without compression).
    events: each entry is
      {"kind": "pulse", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "amount": a, "out-of-boundary": True/False, "occurs": 1/0/'⊥'}, or
      {"kind": "process", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "carrier inside": True/False, "law": 'constant'/'linear'/'bilinear',
       "reads": (position…), "coefficient": k, "bias": b, "cert_eff": 1/0/'⊥', "cert_read": 1/0/'⊥'}.
    links: [(source node, target node, certification)]; a node is 'state:position:beat' or an event identifier.
    closed_graph: whether the evidence graph is closed, which must be declared by the evidence protocol (True/False); if it is undeclared (None), nothing is compiled."""
    if closed_graph not in (True, False):
        raise ValueError("closure of the evidence graph not declared")
    import field_final as FF
    pulses = tuple(FF.Pulse(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["amount"], e.get("out-of-boundary", True), _tri(e.get("occurs", 1)))
                   for e in events if e["kind"] == "pulse")
    procs = tuple(FF.Process(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["carrier inside"], e["law"], tuple(e.get("reads", ())),
                             e.get("coefficient", F(0)), e.get("bias", F(0)), _tri(e.get("cert_eff", 1)), _tri(e.get("cert_read", 1)))
                  for e in events if e["kind"] == "process")
    lk = tuple(FF.Link(a, b, _tri(c)) for a, b, c in links)
    return FF.Spec(tuple(sites), tuple(initial), tuple(window), pulses, procs, lk, _tri(identity), _tri(trace_history),
                   closed_graph, eps, tuple(T))

def j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events):
    """The same run under this paper's original-mechanism semantics (post is the identity): at each beat every process computes its component from the pre-beat state by its law, occurring pulses are added, and all components are booked.
    Every number entering the computation is first normalized by exact of [S2] (a decimal is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation), the same interpretation as in the upstream evaluator."""
    from field_final import exact
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(sites, initial)}
    traj = []
    for t in range(window[0] - 1, window[-1] + 1):
        before = dict(st)
        add = {x: F(0) for x in sites}
        for e in events:
            if e["beat"] != t:
                continue
            if e["kind"] == "pulse":
                if e.get("occurs", 1) == 1:
                    add[e["target"]] += exact(e["amount"])
            else:
                rd = e.get("reads", ()); k = exact(e.get("coefficient", F(0))); b = exact(e.get("bias", F(0)))
                v = b if e["law"] == "constant" else (b + k * sum(before[q] for q in rd) if e["law"] == "linear"
                                                     else b + k * before[rd[0]] * before[rd[1]])
                add[e["target"]] += v
        st = {x: before[x] + add[x] for x in sites}
        if t in window:
            traj.append(dict(st))
    return traj

def j18_check(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """The three conditions of Theorem 18-4(4), evaluated through the [S2] evaluator (field_final.check, run):
    (A) an F candidate event table can be generated: j18_spec compiles (whether the evidence graph is closed is declared by the evidence protocol), and field_final.check accepts the raw certifications and raw events under D20;
         when D20 rejects (e.g. the action certification is undetermined, even for a zero component), F is not run and the R layer is reported separately as pending;
    (B) the numerical trajectory of the original run is reproduced: the post-states at each beat of the same original-mechanism run equal, position by position, the post-states at each beat reported by the F run;
    (C) witnesses and qualification are preserved: field_final.run computes Trace_F, Part_F, HDyn_F and Q_F of D19 and the per-event atoms."""
    import field_final as FF
    try:
        spec = j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links, identity, trace_history, closed_graph)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "must be declared by the evidence protocol before evaluation", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        FF.check(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        out = FF.run(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject (left the domain during the run): %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 1,
                "trajectory": "not run", "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    f_traj = [rep["after"] for rep in out["reports"]]
    raw = j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events)
    traj = 1 if [{x: F(v) for x, v in a.items()} for a in f_traj] == raw else 0
    evs = [dict(id=e["id"], c=e["delta"], Act_F=_untri(e["act"]), Cond_F=_untri(e["cond"]), HistCond_F=_untri(e["histcond"]))
           for rep in out["reports"] for e in rep["events"]]
    return {"D20": "accept", "R": "—", "event table": 1, "trajectory": traj, "Trace_F": _untri(out["trace"]), "Part_F": _untri(out["part"]),
            "HDyn_F": _untri(out["hdyn"]), "Q_F": _untri(out["Q"]), "events": evs, "F trajectory": f_traj, "original-mechanism trajectory": raw}

def nonneg_encode(d):
    """Nonnegative encoding of signed depth for entry into the F layer: d = d⁺ − d⁻ with d⁺, d⁻ ≥ 0; returns (encoding, read-back function)."""
    enc = {}
    for x, v in d.items():
        enc[(x, "+")] = v if v > 0 else F(0)
        enc[(x, "−")] = -v if v < 0 else F(0)
    return enc, (lambda e: {x: e[(x, "+")] - e[(x, "−")] for x in d})
~~~

A.3  field\_final.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.4 of *The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation* v1.0, byte-for-byte identical to Appendix A.1 of *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0 (the F-layer evaluator)

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A: F-layer (finite separable layer) evaluator.
Formal simulation, not certification of material causation.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """Each input number is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation; all F-layer arithmetic is carried out exactly over the rationals."""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # Legal numbers: integers, rationals (Fraction), or finite decimal floats
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # In F, certified is the certification of “whether the pulse actually occurs”: 1 = occurs, 0 = does not occur; ⊥ is outside the single-trajectory domain of F and must be enumerated by branch in R
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20: the action certification must be decided; a mechanism with undetermined action is outside the F domain (even when its component is zero)
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # out-of-boundary cause: changes left at this position by certified, effective out-of-boundary action components (pulses, or processes whose carrier is outside the boundary)
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # Count only pulses certified as occurring and effective; a pulse confirmed not to occur contributes nothing and registers no formation or cause
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # Support for the organizational condition: the numerical law depends on the read positions, or the process is a pure read (component not an effective change, |c| ≤ ε); read certification enters as a separate conjunct, and an undetermined value is not truncated to false in advance
            # Functional support is decided by a mathematically nonzero partial derivative (coefficient and partner reading exactly nonzero), not conflated with the effective-change threshold ε
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # Formation of a new element: with its carrier inside B it is formation by in-field continuation; with its carrier outside the boundary it is formation by signal
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # An effective component of a process whose carrier is outside the boundary is a change left by a signal, whether the element is new or old and whether the component is positive or negative
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # defensive check: an undetermined action certification is already excluded from the domain by check()
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # Depth must not be negative: an exact updated value below zero leaves the domain; no truncation to zero, no tolerance
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

A.4  snn\_core.py  Upstream frozen program: Appendix A.1 of *The Adjudication Form of Field Concretization for Candidate Hosts* v1.0 (the frozen SNN instance s1, that is, the frozen program of Instance P of that paper)

~~~python
"""Frozen SNN instance s1: a replayable SNN instance (program host). Exact rational arithmetic, Python standard library, deterministic, replayable event by event.
Host instance χ_SNN = (P, L, B, W):
  P this program; L the algorithmic level (discrete time steps); B = {4 input neurons, 2 output LIF neurons, 8 synapses}; W the declared window.
  Input-neuron firing is fixed by an external stimulus sequence (out-of-boundary signal); the teaching current teach is injected into output 0 only during the formation phase (out-of-boundary signal).
  Output neurons: LIF, v ← v·LEAK + Σ w_ij·s_i + I_ext; when v ≥ θ the neuron fires and resets to 0.
  Plasticity: pair-based STDP (trace form): presynaptic traces x_i and postsynaptic traces y_j are multiplied by DEC each step and incremented by 1 on firing;
    when postsynaptic neuron j fires (via the backpropagated signal reaching the synapse), w_ij += A_PLUS·x_i; when presynaptic neuron i fires, w_ij -= A_MINUS·y_j;
    weights are clipped to [0, W_MAX]. Optional relaxation toward baseline (RELAX, default 0).
  Intervention switches: plastic (plasticity on/off); bap (whether postsynaptic firing propagates back to the synapse; when off, STDP does not see postsynaptic firing, i.e. the route is cut).
Trace-Response: above-baseline weight z_ij = w_ij − W0.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import hashlib
N_IN, N_OUT = 4, 2
W0 = F(1, 4)
LEAK, THETA, DEC = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 2)
A_PLUS, A_MINUS, W_MAX = F(1, 8), F(1, 16), F(2)
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0)}

def init(w=None):
    return dict(w=[[W0 if w is None else w[i][j] for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)],
                v=[F(0)] * N_OUT, x=[F(0)] * N_IN, y=[F(0)] * N_OUT)

def step(st, inp, teach=(0, 0), plastic=True, bap=True, relax=F(0)):
    """One step: returns (new state, event record). The event record lists presynaptic firing, postsynaptic firing and the plasticity components of each synapse (positive, negative, relaxation)."""
    w = [row[:] for row in st['w']]
    s_in = PAT[inp]
    v = [st['v'][j] * LEAK + sum(w[i][j] * s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)) + teach[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    s_out = [1 if v[j] >= THETA else 0 for j in range(N_OUT)]
    v = [F(0) if s_out[j] else v[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * DEC + s_in[i] for i in range(N_IN)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * DEC + s_out[j] for j in range(N_OUT)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(N_IN):
        for j in range(N_OUT):
            pos = A_PLUS * x[i] if (plastic and bap and s_out[j]) else F(0)
            neg = -A_MINUS * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else F(0)
            rel = -relax * (w[i][j] - W0) if plastic else F(0)
            nw = min(W_MAX, max(F(0), w[i][j] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[i][j])
            w[i][j] = nw
    ev = dict(inp=inp, teach=teach, s_in=s_in, s_out=tuple(s_out), comp=comp)
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), ev

def run(hist, st=None, **kw):
    """hist: [(input name, teaching current)]. Returns (final state, event log)."""
    st = st or init()
    log = []
    for inp, teach in hist:
        st, ev = step(st, inp, teach, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
    return st, log

def log_hash(log):
    """Fingerprint of the frozen log: SHA-256 of the events serialized in a fixed order."""
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for ev in log:
        h.update(repr((ev['inp'], ev['teach'], ev['s_in'], ev['s_out'],
                       sorted((k, tuple(str(c) for c in v)) for k, v in ev['comp'].items()))).encode())
    return h.hexdigest()

def z(st):
    return [[st['w'][i][j] - W0 for j in range(N_OUT)] for i in range(N_IN)]
~~~

A.5  ann20f\_models.py  Library of this paper: SNN s1/s2, the gradient-trained network and its variants, and the declared models of the KV cache and fast weights

~~~python
"""The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Artificial Neural Networks v1.0, appendix program: declared model library. Standard library only; every number is a fractions.Fraction; no random numbers
(random histories are generated by ann20f_run.py with fixed seeds and serve only as samples for finite verification).
1. SNN (program host, local plasticity)
  snn_beat       independent implementation with the same per-beat order as the frozen snn_core.step under the s1 parameters; s2 extensions can also be switched on: letter A2 (the gradient network uses C instead), same-beat competition (WTA),
                 global reset after firing (GRESET), relaxation RELAX. s1 = all extensions off; s2 does not rewrite the s1 verdicts.
  SNNJH          host interface to jhc_core of [17] Appendix A.1: X contains the 8 weight positions and v, x, y (activity and residue are both listed as carriers, so that the three-way split can be made).
  snn_jxs        host interface to jxs_core of [18] Appendix A.1: d = w − W0 (8 positions), r = (v, x, y).
2. Gradient-trained network (isomorphic 4→2 linear units, online delta rule / squared-error SGD)
  GDJH, gd_jxs   the write is decomposed into two processes ([declared]): TGT = η·s_i·t_j (external-target term, does not read the Trace-Response) and ERR = −η·s_i·y_j (error self-term, reads the Trace-Response).
  GDAdaJH        law-layer variant: per-position accumulator acc (rational AdaGrad type: step size η/(1 + acc)); a history-controlled law-layer component ([18] D18-11).
  GDClockJH      clock-controlled variant: the step size halves after beat K of the beat clock clk (clock-controlled law layer).
  SelfTrainJH    self-referential target variant: t_j = [y_j ≥ 1/2] (pseudo-label); the target is generated by the host's own activity.
3. Inference-time state carriers
  FWHebbJH       outer-product fast weights (linear attention): S ← λS + v kᵀ; the write does not read S, shallowing reads S directly.
  FWDeltaJH      delta-rule fast weights: S ← S + β(v − S k)kᵀ; the write reads S through the retrieval activity S k.
  KVJH           two-layer append-only KV cache (scalar keys and values, normalized attention over nonnegative scores; the layer-1 write reads only the current-beat input, the layer-2 write reads the layer-1 attention output).
All parameters are constructed values (dimensionless rationals), not measured values.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
import jhc_core as JH
import jxs_core as JX
ZERO = F(0)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- SNN
PAT = {'A': (1, 1, 0, 0), 'B': (0, 0, 1, 1), 'AB': (1, 1, 1, 1), 'none': (0, 0, 0, 0), 'A2': (0, 1, 1, 0), 'C': (1, 0, 0, 0), 'I1': (0, 1, 0, 0), 'I2': (0, 0, 1, 0)}
S1 = dict(N_IN=4, N_OUT=2, W0=F(1, 4), LEAK=F(3, 4), THETA=F(1), DEC=F(1, 2), A_PLUS=F(1, 8), A_MINUS=F(1, 16),
          W_MAX=F(2), RELAX=F(0), WTA=False, GRESET=False)

def params(**kw):
    p = dict(S1)
    p.update(kw)
    return p

def parse(sig):
    """Letters: 'A', 'B', 'AB', 'A2', 'rest' (= none); the suffix '+T0'/'+T1' injects teaching current 1 into output 0/1 (an out-of-boundary signal)."""
    teach = [ZERO, ZERO]
    name = sig
    if '+T' in sig:
        name, j = sig.split('+T')
        teach[int(j)] = F(1)
    if name == 'rest':
        name = 'none'
    return PAT[name], tuple(teach)

def snn_init(p, w=None):
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    return dict(w={(i, j): (p['W0'] if w is None else w[(i, j)]) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)},
                v=[ZERO] * m, x=[ZERO] * n, y=[ZERO] * m)

def snn_gate(p, vt):
    m = len(vt)
    g = [1 if vt[j] >= p['THETA'] else 0 for j in range(m)]
    if p['WTA'] and sum(g) > 1:
        top = max(vt[j] for j in range(m) if g[j])
        g = [1 if (g[j] and vt[j] == top) else 0 for j in range(m)]
    return g

def snn_beat(p, st, sig, plastic=True, bap=True):
    """One beat. Execution order as in snn_core.step (integration reads the pre-beat w → gating → reset → trace update → potentiation, depression, relaxation → clipping)."""
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    s_in, teach = parse(sig)
    w = dict(st['w'])
    vt = [st['v'][j] * p['LEAK'] + sum(w[(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
    g = snn_gate(p, vt)
    if p['GRESET'] and any(g):
        v = [ZERO] * m
    else:
        v = [ZERO if g[j] else vt[j] for j in range(m)]
    x = [st['x'][i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i] for i in range(n)]
    y = [st['y'][j] * p['DEC'] + g[j] for j in range(m)]
    comp = {}
    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(m):
            pos = p['A_PLUS'] * x[i] if (plastic and bap and g[j]) else ZERO
            neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y[j] if (plastic and s_in[i] and bap) else ZERO
            rel = -p['RELAX'] * (w[(i, j)] - p['W0']) if plastic else ZERO
            nw = min(p['W_MAX'], max(ZERO, w[(i, j)] + pos + neg + rel))
            comp[(i, j)] = (pos, neg, rel, nw - w[(i, j)])
            w[(i, j)] = nw
    return dict(w=w, v=v, x=x, y=y), dict(sig=sig, s_in=s_in, g=tuple(g), vt=tuple(vt), comp=comp)

def snn_run(p, hist, st=None, **kw):
    st = st or snn_init(p)
    log, sts = [], [st]
    for s in hist:
        st, ev = snn_beat(p, st, s, **kw)
        log.append(ev)
        sts.append(st)
    return sts, log

def z_of(p, st, ij):
    return st['w'][ij] - p['W0']

def wname(i, j):
    return 'w%d%d' % (i, j)

class SNNJH(JH.Host):
    """[17] interface: X = 8 weight positions + v0, v1 + x0..x3 + y0, y1. Activity y (act): vt, g, new traces xn, yn.
    Processes: PLUS, MINUS, RELAX act on the weights; LIF acts on v; XTR, YTR act on the traces."""
    name = 'SNN'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, p, plastic=True, bap=True):
        self.p, self.plastic, self.bap = p, plastic, bap
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        self.W = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
        self.X = self.W + ['v%d' % j for j in range(m)] + ['x%d' % i for i in range(n)] + ['y%d' % j for j in range(m)]
        big = F(10 ** 6)
        self.lo = {x: (ZERO if x[0] in 'wxy' else -big) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: (p['W_MAX'] if x[0] == 'w' else big) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: (p['W0'] if x[0] == 'w' else ZERO) for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def to_st(self, w):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        return dict(w={(i, j): w[wname(i, j)] for i in range(n) for j in range(m)},
                    v=[w['v%d' % j] for j in range(m)], x=[w['x%d' % i] for i in range(n)], y=[w['y%d' % j] for j in range(m)])
    def from_st(self, st):
        out = {wname(i, j): st['w'][(i, j)] for (i, j) in st['w']}
        out.update({'v%d' % j: st['v'][j] for j in range(len(st['v']))})
        out.update({'x%d' % i: st['x'][i] for i in range(len(st['x']))})
        out.update({'y%d' % j: st['y'][j] for j in range(len(st['y']))})
        return out
    def act(self, w, s):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        s_in, teach = parse(s)
        vt = [w['v%d' % j] * p['LEAK'] + sum(w[wname(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
        g = snn_gate(p, vt)
        y = {}
        for j in range(m):
            y['vt%d' % j] = vt[j]
            y['g%d' % j] = F(g[j])
            y['yn%d' % j] = w['y%d' % j] * p['DEC'] + g[j]
        for i in range(n):
            y['xn%d' % i] = w['x%d' % i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i]
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        p = self.p
        n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
        s_in, _ = parse(s)
        out = []
        anyg = any(y['g%d' % j] for j in range(m))
        for j in range(m):
            g = y['g%d' % j]
            newv = ZERO if (g or (p['GRESET'] and anyg)) else y['vt%d' % j]
            out.append(('LIF', 'v%d' % j, newv - w['v%d' % j]))
            out.append(('YTR', 'y%d' % j, y['yn%d' % j] - w['y%d' % j]))
        for i in range(n):
            out.append(('XTR', 'x%d' % i, y['xn%d' % i] - w['x%d' % i]))
            for j in range(m):
                x = wname(i, j)
                pos = p['A_PLUS'] * y['xn%d' % i] if (self.plastic and self.bap and y['g%d' % j]) else ZERO
                neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y['yn%d' % j] if (self.plastic and s_in[i] and self.bap) else ZERO
                rel = -p['RELAX'] * (w[x] - p['W0']) if self.plastic else ZERO
                out += [('PLUS', x, pos), ('MINUS', x, neg), ('RELAX', x, rel)]
        return out

def snn_jxs(p, plastic=True):
    """[18] interface: d = w − W0; r = (v, x, y); post = per-position clipping (LP)."""
    n, m = p['N_IN'], p['N_OUT']
    pos_ = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
    def read(d, r, s):
        s_in, teach = parse(s)
        vt = [r['v'][j] * p['LEAK'] + sum((p['W0'] + d[wname(i, j)]) * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) + teach[j] for j in range(m)]
        g = snn_gate(p, vt)
        y = {'vt%d' % j: vt[j] for j in range(m)}
        y.update({'g%d' % j: F(g[j]) for j in range(m)})
        y.update({'xn%d' % i: r['x'][i] * p['DEC'] + s_in[i] for i in range(n)})
        y.update({'yn%d' % j: r['y'][j] * p['DEC'] + g[j] for j in range(m)})
        y['s_in'] = s_in
        return y
    def comps(d, y, s):
        out = {}
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                pos = p['A_PLUS'] * y['xn%d' % i] if (plastic and y['g%d' % j]) else ZERO
                neg = -p['A_MINUS'] * y['yn%d' % j] if (plastic and y['s_in'][i]) else ZERO
                rel = -p['RELAX'] * d[wname(i, j)] if plastic else ZERO
                out[wname(i, j)] = [('PLUS', pos), ('MINUS', neg), ('RELAX', rel)]
        return out
    def post(dn, do):
        return {x: min(p['W_MAX'] - p['W0'], max(-p['W0'], v)) for x, v in dn.items()}
    def resid(r, y):
        anyg = any(y['g%d' % j] for j in range(m))
        v = [ZERO if (y['g%d' % j] or (p['GRESET'] and anyg)) else y['vt%d' % j] for j in range(m)]
        return dict(v=v, x=[y['xn%d' % i] for i in range(n)], y=[y['yn%d' % j] for j in range(m)])
    return JX.Host(pos_, read, comps, post, resid)

def snn_r0(p):
    return dict(v=[ZERO] * p['N_OUT'], x=[ZERO] * p['N_IN'], y=[ZERO] * p['N_OUT'])

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- gradient-trained network
GD = dict(N_IN=4, N_OUT=2, W0=F(1, 4), ETA=F(1, 4))

def gparse(sig):
    """Letters: 'A', 'B', 'A2', 'AB', 'rest'; a suffix such as ':t10' gives the target (t0, t1) (an out-of-boundary teaching signal); letters without a suffix have no target and no write."""
    if ':t' in sig:
        name, t = sig.split(':t')
        tgt = (F(int(t[0])), F(int(t[1])))
    else:
        name, tgt = sig, None
    if name == 'rest':
        name = 'none'
    return PAT[name], tgt

class GDJH(JH.Host):
    """Linear 4→2, y_j = Σ_i w_ij s_i; a beat with a target writes TGT_ij = η s_i t_j and ERR_ij = −η s_i y_j (their sum is one squared-error SGD step).
    inside: whether the optimizer is declared inside B (affects only the field-type registration, not the run)."""
    name = 'GD'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None, inside=True):
        self.g = dict(g)
        if eta is not None:
            self.g['ETA'] = eta
        self.inside = inside
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        self.X = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
        self.lo = {x: F(-10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: self.g['W0'] for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def act(self, w, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        y = {'y%d' % j: sum(w[wname(i, j)] * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) for j in range(m)}
        return y
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA']
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        out = []
        if tgt is None:
            return out
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                eta = self.rate(w, i, j)
                out.append(('TGT', wname(i, j), eta * s_in[i] * tgt[j]))
                out.append(('ERR', wname(i, j), -eta * s_in[i] * y['y%d' % j]))
        return out

class GDAdaJH(GDJH):
    """History-controlled law layer: X also contains acc_ij; step size η/(1 + acc_ij); on a beat with a target, acc_ij += g² (g = s_i(t_j − y_j)). Φ does not read acc (hidden law layer)."""
    name = 'GDAda'
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        acc = ['a%d%d' % (i, j) for i in range(self.g['N_IN']) for j in range(self.g['N_OUT'])]
        self.X = self.X + acc
        for a in acc:
            self.lo[a], self.hi[a], self.ground[a], self.orient[a] = ZERO, F(10 ** 6), ZERO, 1
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA'] / (1 + w['a%d%d' % (i, j)])
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is None:
            return out
        for i in range(self.g['N_IN']):
            for j in range(self.g['N_OUT']):
                gr = s_in[i] * (tgt[j] - y['y%d' % j])
                out.append(('ACC', 'a%d%d' % (i, j), gr * gr))
        return out

class GDClockJH(GDJH):
    """Clock-controlled law layer: X also contains the beat clock clk (+1 every beat; reads neither the Trace-Response nor the activity); the step size halves once clk ≥ K."""
    name = 'GDClock'
    def __init__(self, K, g=GD, eta=None):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        self.K = K
        self.X = self.X + ['clk']
        self.lo['clk'], self.hi['clk'], self.ground['clk'], self.orient['clk'] = ZERO, F(10 ** 6), ZERO, 1
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def rate(self, w, i, j):
        return self.g['ETA'] if w['clk'] < self.K else self.g['ETA'] / 2
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        return super().comps(w, y, s) + [('CLK', 'clk', F(1))]

class SelfTrainJH(GDJH):
    """Self-referential target: on a beat without an out-of-boundary target, the write uses t_j = [y_j ≥ 1/2] as its target (the target is generated by the host's own activity)."""
    name = 'SelfTrain'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is not None:
            return super().comps(w, y, s)
        if sum(s_in) == 0:
            return []
        out = []
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                t = F(1) if y['y%d' % j] >= F(1, 2) else ZERO
                out.append(('PSEUDO', wname(i, j), self.g['ETA'] * s_in[i] * (t - y['y%d' % j])))
        return out

def gd_jxs(g=GD):
    n, m = g['N_IN'], g['N_OUT']
    pos_ = [wname(i, j) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]
    def read(d, r, s):
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        y = {'y%d' % j: sum((g['W0'] + d[wname(i, j)]) * s_in[i] for i in range(n)) for j in range(m)}
        y['s_in'], y['tgt'] = s_in, tgt
        return y
    def comps(d, y, s):
        out = {}
        if y['tgt'] is None:
            return out
        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(m):
                out[wname(i, j)] = [('TGT', g['ETA'] * y['s_in'][i] * y['tgt'][j]),
                                    ('ERR', -g['ETA'] * y['s_in'][i] * y['y%d' % j])]
        return out
    return JX.Host(pos_, read, comps)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- inference-time state carriers
TOK = {'a': ((F(1), F(0)), (F(1), F(0))), 'b': ((F(0), F(1)), (F(0), F(1))), 'c': ((F(1), F(1)), (F(0), F(1)))}

class FWBase(JH.Host):
    grid = None
    def __init__(self):
        self.X = ['S%d%d' % (a, b) for a in range(2) for b in range(2)]
        self.lo = {x: F(-10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    def kv(self, s):
        return TOK[s] if s in TOK else ((ZERO, ZERO), (ZERO, ZERO))
    def act(self, w, s):
        k, _v = self.kv(s)
        return {'r%d' % a: sum(w['S%d%d' % (a, b)] * k[b] for b in range(2)) for a in range(2)}

class FWHebbJH(FWBase):
    """S ← λS + v kᵀ: WRITE = v_a k_b (reads only the current-beat input), DECAY = −(1 − λ)S_ab (direct read of its own position)."""
    name = 'FWHebb'
    def __init__(self, lam=F(3, 4)):
        super().__init__()
        self.lam = lam
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        k, v = self.kv(s)
        out = []
        for a in range(2):
            for b in range(2):
                x = 'S%d%d' % (a, b)
                out += [('WRITE', x, v[a] * k[b]), ('DECAY', x, -(1 - self.lam) * w[x])]
        return out

class FWDeltaJH(FWBase):
    """S ← S + β(v − S k)kᵀ: DELTA = β(v_a − r_a)k_b, where r = S k is the retrieval activity."""
    name = 'FWDelta'
    def __init__(self, beta=F(1, 2)):
        super().__init__()
        self.beta = beta
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        k, v = self.kv(s)
        return [('DELTA', 'S%d%d' % (a, b), self.beta * (v[a] - y['r%d' % a]) * k[b]) for a in range(2) for b in range(2)]

EMB = {'p': F(1), 'q': F(2), 'u': F(3)}

class KVJH(JH.Host):
    """Two-layer append-only KV cache with capacity N. X: n (counter, number of entries written), L1 keys and values k1_i, v1_i, L2 keys and values k2_i, v2_i (i < N).
    Score sc = key × query (both nonnegative); attention output = Σ sc·value / Σ sc (including the current-beat entry). Layer 1: key = value = input embedding e;
    layer 2: key = value = layer-1 attention output o1 (reads the existing layer-1 cache). With layers = 1 there is only layer 1."""
    name = 'KV'
    grid = None
    def __init__(self, N=5, layers=2):
        self.N, self.layers = N, layers
        self.X = ['n'] + ['%s%d_%d' % (kv, l, i) for l in range(1, layers + 1) for kv in 'kv' for i in range(N)]
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.hi = {x: F(10 ** 6) for x in self.X}
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in self.X}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in self.X}
        self.init = dict(self.ground)
    @staticmethod
    def attend(keys, vals, q):
        sc = [k * q for k in keys]
        tot = sum(sc)
        return sum(a * v for a, v in zip(sc, vals)) / tot if tot else ZERO
    def act(self, w, s):
        n = int(w['n'])
        e = EMB.get(s)
        if e is None or n >= self.N:
            return {'o1': ZERO, 'o2': ZERO, 'on': F(0)}
        k1 = [w['k1_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [e]
        v1 = [w['v1_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [e]
        o1 = self.attend(k1, v1, e)
        y = {'o1': o1, 'on': F(1), 'o2': ZERO}
        if self.layers >= 2:
            k2 = [w['k2_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [o1]
            v2 = [w['v2_%d' % i] for i in range(n)] + [o1]
            y['o2'] = self.attend(k2, v2, o1)
        return y
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n = int(w['n'])
        e = EMB.get(s)
        if e is None or n >= self.N:
            return []
        out = [('CNT', 'n', F(1)), ('GEN1k', 'k1_%d' % n, e), ('GEN1v', 'v1_%d' % n, e)]
        if self.layers >= 2:
            out += [('GEN2k', 'k2_%d' % n, y['o1']), ('GEN2v', 'v2_%d' % n, y['o1'])]
        return out
    def readout(self, w, q, layer=1):
        n = int(w['n'])
        ks = [w['k%d_%d' % (layer, i)] for i in range(n)]
        vs = [w['v%d_%d' % (layer, i)] for i in range(n)]
        return self.attend(ks, vs, q)

class GD1JH(GDJH):
    """Single-process declaration (Δ_GD-1): SGD_ij = η s_i (t_j − y_j) counts as one process; same run as GDJH (Δ_GD-2: two processes, TGT and ERR)."""
    name = 'GD1'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        n, m = self.g['N_IN'], self.g['N_OUT']
        s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        if tgt is None:
            return []
        return [('SGD', wname(i, j), self.rate(w, i, j) * s_in[i] * (tgt[j] - y['y%d' % j])) for i in range(n) for j in range(m)]

class GDStepClockJH(GDClockJH):
    """Step clock: clk advances by 1 only on beats that execute an optimization step (letter with a target suffix, zero-gradient steps included); idle-wait beats do not advance it."""
    name = 'GDStepClock'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = GDJH.comps(self, w, y, s)
        _s_in, tgt = gparse(s)
        return out + ([('CLK', 'clk', F(1))] if tgt is not None else [])

class GD2JH(GDJH):
    """Separately built implementation: two channels that the algorithmic level executes separately, that can be switched off independently and that contribute to a common post-state: the teaching channel TGT (reads the external target) and the error channel ERR (reads the forward output),
    each with its own switch, tgt_on and err_on, so that each term can be intervened on separately. With both channels on, the run is identical to that of GD1JH beat by beat."""
    name = 'GD2'
    def __init__(self, g=GD, eta=None, tgt_on=True, err_on=True):
        super().__init__(g, eta)
        self.tgt_on, self.err_on = tgt_on, err_on
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        return [(p, x, c) for (p, x, c) in out if (p == 'TGT' and self.tgt_on) or (p == 'ERR' and self.err_on)]

class GDIdleClockJH(GDClockJH):
    """For the counterexample: the clock advances by 1 only on idle-wait beats that execute no optimization step (a legitimate construction outside the three write-rate candidates)."""
    name = 'GDIdleClock'
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        out = super().comps(w, y, s)
        if gparse(s)[1] is not None:
            return [c for c in out if c[0] != 'CLK']
        return out
~~~

A.6  ann20f\_run.py  Run entry: verifies C0–C8

~~~python
"""The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Artificial Neural Networks v1.0, appendix program: run entry for all verifications.
Run: python3 ann20f_run.py (in a directory containing only the files of Appendix A)
Sections: C0 upstream programs and cross-check; C1 SNN (s1, s2), seven steps; C2 gradient-trained network, seven steps; C3 inference phase;
    C4 cross-host conditional theorems and predictions (in-model values); C5 correspondence with the biological side ([19]); C6 Field Induction engineering interface and resources; C7 replay and determinism.
Each item prints “pass” or “fail”; a summary at the end; the exit code is 1 if any item fails. Standard library only; all numbers are Fraction; random sampling uses fixed seeds.
Finite verification covers only the listed generated domains; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text."""
import hashlib, os, random, sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))

def fl(x):
    return '%.6f' % float(x)

def sha_file(p):
    return hashlib.sha256(open(p, 'rb').read()).hexdigest()

REST = ['rest'] * 8

        'jxs_core.py': '69614a9f07b390075b2d2dd7c340265812e6ff276e3cd6c8b2f685f28a6f3580',
        'field_final.py': 'dfd11fe6866a508542363f9d32bd3bcf1e2f2917445ad61b8fcda78ab859acd1',

def sc_name(s_in):
    return [k for k, v in SC.PAT.items() if v == s_in][0]

def duipai(hist):
    st = SC.init()
    sts, log = M.snn_run(P1, hist)
    bad = 0
    for k, s in enumerate(hist):
        s_in, teach = M.parse(s)
        st, ev = SC.step(st, sc_name(s_in), teach=teach)
        my = sts[k + 1]
        if ([[st['w'][i][j] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)] != [[my['w'][(i, j)] for j in range(2)] for i in range(4)]
                or st['v'] != my['v'] or st['x'] != my['x'] or st['y'] != my['y']
                or any(ev['comp'][ij] != log[k]['comp'][ij] for ij in ev['comp'])):
            bad += 1
    return bad

badr = sum(duipai(h) for h in rhist)

~~~python
      (sum(e['g'][0] for e in l_with), fl(z20w), sum(e['g'][0] for e in l_er), fl(z20e)), z20w > EPS and abs(z20e) <= EPS)

def by_proc(host, states, sigs, T, eps):
    """Effective-difference witnesses by process (combining only avail_at, comp_of and hc_status of [17] Appendix A.1, with the same evaluation rule as hdyn_cert):
    returns {process: (beat, target, A branch, D branch)}, taking for each process its first witness in the window."""
    got = {}
    for k, s in enumerate(sigs):
        w = states[k]
        U = JH.avail_at(host, states, k, T, eps)
        if not U:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s)
        for (p, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            if x not in T or p in got:
                continue
            c = JH.comp_of(host, w, y, s, p, x)
            if abs(c) <= eps:
                continue
            st_, wt = JH.hc_status(host, w, s, p, x, U, eps)
            if st_ == 'effective difference certified':
                got[p] = (k, x, wt[2], wt[3])
    return got

def relax_regimes(rho, Lmax):
    pr = M.params(RELAX=rho)
    Hr = M.SNNJH(pr)
    out = []
    for L in range(Lmax):
        st = M.snn_run(pr, FORM + ['rest'] * L)[0][-1]
        z = min(M.z_of(pr, st, (0, 0)), M.z_of(pr, st, (1, 0)))
        _a, la = M.snn_run(pr, WIN, st)
        e = dict(st)
        e['w'] = {k: pr['W0'] for k in st['w']}
        _b, lb = M.snn_run(pr, WIN, e)
        stj = Hr.from_st(st)
        g = by_proc(Hr, JH.run(Hr, stj, WIN), WIN, ['w00', 'w10'], EPS)
        pa_ = JH.part(Hr, stj, ['w00', 'w10'], ['A'], EPS)
        out.append(dict(L=L, z=z, s1=sum(x['g'][0] for x in la), s0=sum(x['g'][0] for x in lb), wit=g, part=pa_))
    return out

reg = relax_regimes(F(1, 16), 60)

LR = next(o['L'] for o in reg if o['z'] <= EPS)
seg1 = all(o['s1'] != o['s0'] and 'PLUS' in o['wit'] and o['wit']['PLUS'][3] == 0 for o in reg if o['L'] < LP)
seg2 = all(o['s1'] == o['s0'] and not o['wit'] and o['part'] is not None and o['part'][1] == 'vt0' for o in reg if LP <= o['L'] < LR)

mono_ = all(zs_[k + 1] < zs_[k] for k in range(len(zs_) - 1))

      (LP, LP, LR, LR, LD8), seg1 and seg2 and seg8 and LP < LR)

# ---- Step 3  Set organization

hyp, compo = JH.org_graph_exact(Hs2, w_after, ['w00', 'w10', 'w20'], ['A', 'A2'], EPS)

    certs[x_] = (dep_ > EPS, sigf, lb_ >= TAU_W)
PROBE = {'w00': 'C', 'w10': 'I1', 'w20': 'I2'}
k_res = {}
for x_, pr_ in PROBE.items():
    i_ = int(x_[1])
    dom = [P2['W0'] + F(k, 32) for k in range(0, 57)]          # positive depth domain [W0, W_MAX]
    def e_of(wv):
        st_ = dict(Hs2.init)
        st_[x_] = wv
        return abs(Hs2.act(st_, pr_)['vt0'] - Hs2.act(Hs2.init, pr_)['vt0'])
    es = [e_of(wv) for wv in dom]
    k1 = all((wv == P2['W0']) == (wv - P2['W0'] == 0) for wv in dom) and all(wv - P2['W0'] > 0 for wv in dom[1:])
    k2 = all((dom[a] < dom[b]) == (es[a] < es[b]) and (dom[a] == dom[b]) == (es[a] == es[b]) for a in range(len(dom)) for b in range(len(dom)))
    k3 = True

    hid = [q for q in ('A', 'A2', 'B') if M.PAT[q][i_] == 0]
    same_ord = all(sorted(range(len(dom)), key=lambda k: (lambda st_: abs(Hs2.act(st_, q)['vt0'] - Hs2.act(Hs2.init, q)['vt0']))(dict(Hs2.init, **{x_: dom[k]}))) == list(range(len(dom))) for q in ('A', 'A2') if M.PAT[q][i_])
    k_res[x_] = (k1, k2, k3, same_ord, hid)
check('C1i″ Step 3 element certificates K1–K3 ([17] Definitions 3′ and 4′): each position takes a fixed single-carrier probe (w00: C, w10: I1, w20: I2) and the read-out vt0 (non-saturating integration drive), with the other carriers at the ground state on the positive depth domain [W0, W_MAX]; write condition 𝒲 = a teaching write containing that input at resting residue (probe + teaching 0). Per position (K1, K2, K3, same order relation under those of the jointly declared probes A, A2 that contain the input, declared probes hidden from the position) = %s—no order conflict; only the hidden positions (probes not containing the input) must be reported separately; the element certificates of all three positions hold, and the hypergraph of C1i reads as components of issued elements' % k_res,
      all(v[0] and v[1] and v[2] and v[3] for v in k_res.values()))
check('C1i′ Step 3 element certificates (frame = end of the s2 declared history FORM + REST + W2, probes A, A2, joint setting family = all nonempty subsets of E × depth multiples {0, 1/2, 2}): per position (depth > ε, signal formation, self-retention lower bound ≥ τ_W) = %s—all three positions are elements of this frame, and the components are issued in this frame' % certs,

      (len(hyp), compo), compo == [['w00', 'w10', 'w20']])
E4 = ['w00', 'w10', 'w20', 'w30']
d2030 = [JH.depth(H1, SJ[44], x_) for x_ in ('w20', 'w30')]

Demonstration of candidate-position relations (not a verdict on organization components): when the two candidate positions are submitted together, at the end of W (x2, x3 carry residue) they join into %s, and after 8 further rest beats they split into %s—the dependence between candidate positions changes with the residue state' %

      (compo1e, fs(d2030[0]), compo1, compo1r),

def wta(zs, h):

    return sts[-1], sum(e['g'][0] for e in log), sum(e['g'][1] for e in log)

def gd_err_after(eta, n=40):
    H = M.GDJH(eta=eta)
    st = dict(H.init)
    for _ in range(n):
        st = H.step(st, 'A:t10')
    return abs(1 - H.act(st, 'A')['y0'])

def all_comps_T(H, states, sigs, T):
    """Complete-inventory check: the component of every process at every beat of the window on each position of T (recomputed from the pre-beat state). Returns (largest absolute value, number of nonzero subthreshold components, number above threshold)."""
    mx, sub, eff = F(0), 0, 0
    for k, s_ in enumerate(sigs):
        y_ = H.act(states[k], s_)
        for (p_, x_, c_) in H.comps(states[k], y_, s_):
            if x_ in T:
                mx = max(mx, abs(c_))
                sub += 1 if 0 < abs(c_) <= EPS else 0
                eff += 1 if abs(c_) > EPS else 0
    return mx, sub, eff

mxl, subl, effl = all_comps_T(G, SGl, WL, ['w00', 'w10'])

def j18_gd(inside, n_beats=4):
    """Compiled from the real trajectory of the original GD (single process): the closed projection {z00, z10} of output 0 (its writes read only these two positions and the signal; proof in 4.2.6 (5) of the main text).
    At each beat t = 0…n−1, A:t10 is presented: SGD_zi0@t = η(t₀ − y0) = (η − 2ηW0) − η(z00 + z10), a D12 linear law; beat 0 already contains this process (the first-beat error term).
    The target enters as the bias of the law; inside B a pure read process FWD (reads z00, z10, zero component) bears Part. window = beats 1…n−1; beat 0 is the pre-window formation beat."""
    sites = ('z00', 'z10', 'y0')
    eta = F(1, 4)
    ev, links = [], []
    for t in range(n_beats):
        for x in ('z00', 'z10'):
            nm = 'SGD_%s@%d' % (x, t)
            ev.append({'kind': 'process', 'name': nm, 'beat': t, 'target': x, 'carrier inside': inside, 'law': 'linear', 'reads': ('z00', 'z10'),
                       'coefficient': -eta, 'bias': eta - 2 * eta * W0g, 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1})
            if t >= 1:      # beat 0 has no formed position yet, so there is no history path to link (the upstream evaluator accepts only in-window state nodes)
                links += [('state:z00:%d' % t, nm, 1), ('state:z10:%d' % t, nm, 1)]
        nm = 'FWD@%d' % t
        ev.append({'kind': 'process', 'name': nm, 'beat': t, 'target': 'y0', 'carrier inside': True, 'law': 'linear', 'reads': ('z00', 'z10'),
                   'coefficient': F(0), 'bias': F(0), 'cert_eff': 1, 'cert_read': 1})
        if t >= 1:
            links += [('state:z00:%d' % t, nm, 1), ('state:z10:%d' % t, nm, 1)]
    jr = JX.j18_check(sites, (F(0),) * 3, tuple(range(1, n_beats)), EPS, ('z00', 'z10'), ev, links, closed_graph=True)
    return jr, ev, links

(js, evs, lks), (jn, evn, lkn) = j18_gd(True), j18_gd(False)
# Independent comparison: the F trajectory is checked beat by beat and position by position against the original-mechanism GD (jhc interface) (not two executions of the same hand-written table)
gd_traj = JH.run(G, G.init, ['A:t10'] * 4)
f_ok = all(F(js['F trajectory'][k]['z%s0' % i]) == gd_traj[k + 2]['w%s0' % i] - W0g for k in range(3) for i in '01')
pre_ok = F(js['original-mechanism trajectory'][0]['z00']) == gd_traj[2]['w00'] - W0g and gd_traj[1]['w00'] - W0g == F(1, 8)
# Legitimate control: set z00 back to 0 at each beat; the F law value equals the original GD process component in every case
con_ok = True
for k in range(1, 4):
    stc = dict(gd_traj[k], w00=W0g)
    law_v = (F(1, 4) - 2 * F(1, 4) * W0g) - F(1, 4) * ((stc['w00'] - W0g) + (stc['w10'] - W0g))
    con_ok &= law_v == JH.total_comp(G, stc, 'A:t10', 'SGD', 'w00')
# Projection closure: changing the weights of output 1 does not change the components at the output-0 positions
st_c = dict(gd_traj[2], w01=F(7, 8), w11=F(-3, 8))
closed = all(JH.total_comp(G, st_c, 'A:t10', 'SGD', x) == JH.total_comp(G, gd_traj[2], 'A:t10', 'SGD', x) for x in ('w00', 'w10'))

def inner_loop(jr, ev, links, B_sites=('z00', 'z10', 'y0')):
    """[S1] Section 7, checked independently from the event table and the evaluation result: there is an event with Act_F = 1 and HistCond_F = 1 whose carrier is inside B
    and all of whose evidence-path sources are state nodes of Structural Positions inside B."""
    carrier = {e['name']: e['carrier inside'] for e in ev if e['kind'] == 'process'}
    srcs = {}
    for a, b, c in links:
        srcs.setdefault(b, []).append(a)
    for e in jr['events']:
        if e['Act_F'] == 1 and e['HistCond_F'] == 1 and carrier.get(e['id']):
            if all(a.startswith('state:') and a.split(':')[1] in B_sites for a in srcs.get(e['id'], [])):
                return 1
    return 0

ils, iln = inner_loop(js, evs, lks), inner_loop(jn, evn, lkn)

B\_sys: D20 “%s”, Trace\_F = %s, Part\_F = %s, HDyn\_F = %s, Q\_F = %s, in-boundary self-feedback type %d; B\_net: Trace\_F = %s, Part\_F = %s, HDyn\_F = %s, Q\_F = %s, in-boundary self-feedback type %d (checked independently from the carriers and paths of the event table)' %

# Step 7: history independence of the destination (full rank) and permanent hiding in the null space (rank-deficient)

def cycle_matrix(pats, eta):
    n = 4
    Mx = [[F(int(i == j)) for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
    for pn in pats:
        s = M.PAT[pn]
        P = [[F(int(i == j)) - eta * s[i] * s[j] for j in range(n)] for i in range(n)]
        Mx = JX.mat_mul(P, Mx)
    return Mx

def inf_norm(A):
    return max(sum(abs(v) for v in row) for row in A)

Mfull = cycle_matrix(['A', 'B', 'A2', 'C'], F(1, 4))
Mk = Mfull
k_c = 1
while inf_norm(Mk) >= 1 and k_c < 64:
    Mk = JX.mat_mul(Mk, Mfull)
    k_c += 1
check('C2k Step 7 (full-rank alphabet {A, B, A2, C}, η = 1/4): the linear part M of the one-cycle map, raised to the power %d, has ∞-norm %s < 1—the cycle map is a contraction with a unique, globally attracting fixed point: the destination does not depend on the existing Trace-Responses (model certificate of Theorem 20-2 (1))' %
      (k_c, fl(inf_norm(Mk))), inf_norm(Mk) < 1)
Mdef = cycle_matrix(['A', 'B', 'A2', 'AB'], F(1, 4))
nul = [F(1), F(-1), F(1), F(-1)]
fixed = all(sum(Mdef[i][j] * nul[j] for j in range(4)) == nul[i] for i in range(4))
Acl = Mdef
Cread = [list(map(F, M.PAT[s])) for s in ('A', 'B', 'A2', 'AB')]
hid_tr = JX.hidden_forever_lti(Acl, Cread, nul)
hid_c = JX.hidden_forever_lti(Acl, [list(map(F, M.PAT['C']))], nul)

      (kvres[1][0], kvres[2][0], kvres[2][1][2], kvres[2][1][3], kvres[2][1][6], kvres['L2'][0]),

      [fs(v) for v in dil], all(dil[k + 1] < dil[k] for k in range(4)) and wk['k1_0'] == F(1))

      (fw['FWHebb'][0], fw['FWHebb'][1][2], fs(fw['FWHebb'][1][-3]), fs(fw['FWHebb'][1][-2]),

rd = {}
for Hx in (Hd, Hh_):

    s1 = JH.run(Hx, Hx.init, ['a', 'c'])[-1]

check('C3e fast-weight overlap (keys a = (1, 0) and c = (1, 1) overlap in the first dimension; values a → (1, 0) and c → (0, 1) conflict): under the delta rule (β = 1/2), writing c changes the retrieval of a from (%s, %s) to (%s, %s) (on the overlap the new overwrites the old); under the outer product (λ = 1) it changes from (%s, %s) to (%s, %s) (crosstalk superposes, the old component is kept)' %
      tuple(fs(v) for v in rd['FWDelta'] + rd['FWHebb']),
      rd['FWDelta'][2] < rd['FWDelta'][0] and rd['FWHebb'][2] == rd['FWHebb'][0] and rd['FWHebb'][3] > rd['FWHebb'][1])
rngf = random.Random(20261108)
fbad = 0
for _ in range(200):
    Sm = [[F(rngf.randint(-4, 4), 4) for _b in range(2)] for _a in range(2)]
    ka, kc = (F(1), F(0)), (F(1), F(rngf.randint(0, 2)))
    vc = (F(rngf.randint(-2, 2), 2), F(rngf.randint(-2, 2), 2))
    beta, lam = F(1, 2), F(rngf.randint(1, 4), 4)
    Sk = lambda S_, k_: [S_[a][0] * k_[0] + S_[a][1] * k_[1] for a in range(2)]
    kk = kc[0] * ka[0] + kc[1] * ka[1]
    skc = Sk(Sm, kc)
    Sd = [[Sm[a][b] + beta * (vc[a] - skc[a]) * kc[b] for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
    Sh = [[lam * Sm[a][b] + vc[a] * kc[b] for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
    dd_ = [Sk(Sd, ka)[a] - Sk(Sm, ka)[a] for a in range(2)]
    dh_ = [Sk(Sh, ka)[a] - Sk(Sm, ka)[a] for a in range(2)]
    fbad += sum(1 for a in range(2) if dd_[a] != beta * kk * (vc[a] - skc[a]))
    fbad += sum(1 for a in range(2) if dh_[a] != (lam - 1) * Sk(Sm, ka)[a] + kk * vc[a])
check('C3f closed form of the retrieval difference in P20-7 (200 random rational states, seed 20261108): delta rule ΔS k_a = β(k_c·k_a)(v_c − S k_c); outer product ΔS k_a = (λ − 1)S k_a + (k_c·k_a)v_c; violations %d—the sign of the old-value component is determined jointly by the projection k_c·k_a, the component of v_c − S k_c along the old-value direction and the decay, not by “overlap with conflicting values” alone' % fbad, fbad == 0)
# ============================================================ C4
print('=' * 60)
print('C4  Cross-host conditional theorems and predictions (in-model values)')
# P20-1 load-bearing observation: the actual single-beat increment of the same new position w20; the two arms differ only in the old position w10 (W0 vs W0 + d), common input A2,
# same residue (v, x, y), same initial value of the new position; no teaching on the SNN side, the same target t0 = 1 on the delta side; coincident activations a_old = a_new = 1.
def snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, w10):
    st_ = M.snn_init(pp)
    st_['v'] = [v0, F(0)]
    st_['x'] = [F(0), x1, x2, F(0)]
    st_['y'] = [y0, F(0)]
    st_['w'][(2, 0)] = w20
    st_['w'][(1, 0)] = w10
    _n, ev_ = M.snn_beat(pp, st_, 'A2')
    return ev_['comp'][(2, 0)][3], ev_['g'][0], ev_['comp'][(2, 0)]

n_dom = n_flip = n_clamp = bad_f = bad_s = 0
min_flip = None
for rho in (F(0), F(1, 16), F(1, 8)):
    pp = M.params(RELAX=rho)
    for v0 in (F(0), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4)):
        for x1 in (F(0), F(1)):
            for x2 in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)):
                for y0 in (F(0), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2)):
                    for w20 in (F(0), F(1, 64), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(1), F(3, 2), F(2)):
                        for dd in (F(1, 32), F(1, 8), F(1, 4), F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(1)):
                            i1, g1, c1 = snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, pp['W0'] + dd)
                            i0, g0_, c0 = snn_inc(pp, v0, x1, x2, y0, w20, pp['W0'])
                            chi = i1 - i0
                            if chi < 0:
                                bad_s += 1
                            raw1, raw0 = sum(c1[:3]), sum(c0[:3])
                            unclamped = 0 <= w20 + raw1 <= pp['W_MAX'] and 0 <= w20 + raw0 <= pp['W_MAX']
                            if unclamped:
                                n_dom += 1
                                if chi != (pp['A_PLUS'] * (x2 * pp['DEC'] + 1) - pp['A_MINUS']) * (g1 - g0_):
                                    bad_f += 1
                                if g1 > g0_:
                                    n_flip += 1
                                    min_flip = chi if min_flip is None else min(min_flip, chi)
                            else:
                                n_clamp += 1

# Counterexample X20-10: when the two arms have different step sizes (each with lower bound 1/8), the delta-side χ can fall on the Candidate A side—D5 must require each pair to share the same actual step size
def gd_inc(eta, w10):
    H_ = M.GDJH(eta=eta)
    st_ = dict(H_.init, w10=w10)
    return H_.step(st_, 'A2:t10')['w20'] - st_['w20']

# Finite verification of the single-beat Theorem 20-3: random states
rng2 = random.Random(20261106)
bad = {'Hebb': 0, 'LMS': 0, 'STDP': 0}
for _ in range(400):
    a_new, a_old = F(1), F(1)
    w_new = F(rng2.randint(0, 32), 32)
    w_old = F(rng2.randint(1, 32), 32)
    base_ = F(rng2.randint(0, 32), 32)
    t = F(rng2.randint(0, 64), 32)
    eta = F(1, 8)
    y_w = base_ + w_new * a_new + w_old * a_old
    y_e = base_ + w_new * a_new
    if not (eta * a_new * y_w > eta * a_new * y_e):
        bad['Hebb'] += 1
    if not (eta * a_new * (t - y_w) < eta * a_new * (t - y_e)):
        bad['LMS'] += 1
    th = F(rng2.randint(1, 64), 32)
    if not ((1 if y_w >= th else 0) >= (1 if y_e >= th else 0)):
        bad['STDP'] += 1
check('C4b finite verification of the single-beat form of Theorem 20-3 (400 random rational states, seed 20261106): c_new = η·a_new·e(y); when the old Trace-Response increases y, the cross term is > 0 for Hebbian e = y, < 0 for error e = t − y and ≥ 0 for threshold gating e = [y ≥ θ]; violations %s' % bad,
      sum(bad.values()) == 0)

# Corollary of P20-5: “savings” of a silent Trace-Response—the interval within [L_P, L_Ret) in which, with two adjacent A presentations as a strong cue, the Trace-Response side fires and the erased side does not

LS = max([L for (L, a_, b_) in sav if a_ > b_] or [REG[0] - 1]) + 1

    c1 = sum(c for _, c in HX.comps(d1, HX.read(d1, r_, 'A2'), 'A2')['w20'])
    c2 = sum(c for _, c in HX.comps(d2, HX.read(d2, r_, 'A2'), 'A2')['w20'])

    g2 = sum(c for _, c in GX.comps(d2, GX.read(d2, None, 'A2:t10'), 'A2:t10')['w20'])

        bad_dn += 1

def predict(d0, d1, v0):
    """Prediction from the read-out map: vt0(A) = λv0 + Σ(the two positions of A), vt0(A2) = λv0 + w10 + w20; only w00 and w10 are changed."""
    return (int(eA['vt'][0] + d0 + d1 >= 1), int(eA2['vt'][0] + d1 >= 1))

      (fs(marg), fs(gap), fs(gap), fs(marg), pn, pbad), pbad == 0 and gap == F(42799, 524288))

      (arms_['directed'][0], arms_['original'][0], arms_['reversed'][0], tuple(fs(c) for c in arms_['directed'][1]), tuple(fs(c) for c in arms_['original'][1]),

# Fate of the rewrite (Theorem 20-7)
e_ = {(0, 0): F(-1, 8), (1, 0): F(1, 8)}
def snn_pair(hist):
    a_ = dict(stA, w=dict(stA['w']))
    for k, v in e_.items():
        a_['w'][k] += v
    sa, la = M.snn_run(P1, hist, a_)
    sb, lb = M.snn_run(P1, hist, stA)
    gates_same = [x['g'] for x in la] == [x['g'] for x in lb]
    return {k: sa[-1]['w'][k] - sb[-1]['w'][k] for k in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (2, 0))}, gates_same
dA, gsA = snn_pair(['A', 'rest', 'rest'] * 8)
dA2, gsA2 = snn_pair(['A2', 'rest', 'rest'] * 6)

check('C8f fate of the rewrite (Theorem 20-7 (1), SNN s1 law, ρ = 0): the same rewrite e = (w00 −1/8, w10 +1/8). Presenting only A: gate sequences of the two arms identical %s ⇒ the rewrite difference is kept unchanged position by position %s; presenting A2: gate sequences differ %s, the rewrite is amplified and recruits a new position (w20 difference %s, w10 difference %s)' %

~~~python
      (gsA, {k: fs(v) for k, v in dA.items()}, not gsA2, fl(dA2[(2, 0)]), fl(dA2[(1, 0)])),
      gsA and dA == {(0, 0): F(-1, 8), (1, 0): F(1, 8), (2, 0): F(0)} and not gsA2 and dA2[(2, 0)] > EPS)
def gd_pair(hist, base):
    a_ = dict(base, w00=base['w00'] - F(1, 8), w10=base['w10'] + F(1, 8))
    ra, rb = JH.run(G, a_, hist)[-1], JH.run(G, base, hist)[-1]
    return [ra['w%d0' % i] - rb['w%d0' % i] for i in range(4)]
gA = gd_pair(['A:t10'] * 30, SG[-1])
gV = gd_pair(['A:t10', 'A2:t10'] * 30, SG[-1])
gS = gd_pair(['A'] * 30, SG[-1])

      ([fs(v) for v in gA], [fl(v) for v in gV], [fs(v) for v in gS]),

rngb = random.Random(20261109)
fb = 0
for _ in range(400):
    pp = M.params(RELAX=F(rngb.randint(0, 2), 16))
    sa_ = M.snn_init(pp)
    sa_['x'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2) for _i in range(4)]
    sb_ = dict(sa_, x=list(sa_['x']), w=dict(sa_['w']))
    sa_['y'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2), F(0)]
    sb_['y'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 3), 2), F(0)]
    for ij in sa_['w']:
        sa_['w'][ij] = F(rngb.randint(4, 40), 32)
        sb_['w'][ij] = F(rngb.randint(4, 40), 32)
    sa_['v'] = sb_['v'] = [F(rngb.randint(0, 4), 8), F(0)]
    sig = rngb.choice(['A', 'A2', 'B', 'AB'])
    _x, ea = M.snn_beat(pp, sa_, sig)
    _x, eb = M.snn_beat(pp, sb_, sig)
    s_in = M.PAT[sig]
    for (i, j) in sa_['w']:
        if j != 0:
            continue
        xp = sa_['x'][i] * pp['DEC'] + s_in[i]
        dg = ea['g'][0] - eb['g'][0]
        dy = sa_['y'][0] - sb_['y'][0]
        dw = sa_['w'][(i, 0)] - sb_['w'][(i, 0)]
        pred = pp['A_PLUS'] * xp * dg - pp['A_MINUS'] * s_in[i] * (pp['DEC'] * dy + dg) - pp['RELAX'] * dw
        fb += sum(ea['comp'][(i, 0)][:3]) - sum(eb['comp'][(i, 0)][:3]) != pred

_sb, lb2 = M.snn_run(P1, ['A', 'A'], base_)

actual2 = sum(la2[1]['comp'][(1, 0)][:3]) - sum(lb2[1]['comp'][(1, 0)][:3])

orthok = [orth[i] - [F(1, 16), F(-1, 16), F(0), F(0)][i] for i in range(4)] == [F(1, 16) / 2 ** 12, F(1, 16) / 2 ** 12, F(0), F(0)]
Mm = cycle_matrix(['A', 'A2'], F(1, 4))
Xv = [list(map(F, M.PAT['A'])), list(map(F, M.PAT['A2']))]
Gm = [[sum(Xv[a][i] * Xv[b][i] for i in range(4)) for b in range(2)] for a in range(2)]
dt = Gm[0][0] * Gm[1][1] - Gm[0][1] * Gm[1][0]
Gi = [[Gm[1][1] / dt, -Gm[0][1] / dt], [-Gm[1][0] / dt, Gm[0][0] / dt]]
PV = [[sum(Xv[a][i] * Gi[a][b] * Xv[b][j] for a in range(2) for b in range(2)) for j in range(4)] for i in range(4)]
Mk_, kb, ka = Mm, None, None
for k in range(1, 40):

    actual_probe = abs(sum(M.PAT['A'][i] * dvec[i] for i in range(4)))
    bound = sum(M.PAT['A']) * inf_norm(JX.mat_mul(Mk_, PV)) * max(abs(v) for v in e0)
    if ka is None and actual_probe <= EPS:
        ka = k
    if kb is None and bound <= EPS:
        kb = k
    Mk_ = JX.mat_mul(Mk_, Mm)

B.1  Runtime Environment and Checksums

The command of B.2 is run with python3 -E -s -B in an empty directory containing only the six files of Appendix A; it exits with code 0 and an empty standard error, and the output is deterministic (random sampling uses fixed seeds; two runs are byte-for-byte identical).
